- **lean-audit**: Use when auditing prose and skill/plugin workflow surfaces — a repo, file, or diff of docs, SKILL.md, agents, references, or extensions — for duplication and waste: restated prose, stale/dead references, oversized or verbose context, per-use/per-mode load cost, and pre-run finishability of staged, iterative, or delegated workflows (orchestrator context growth, tool/hook/log/handoff cost, verification reserve, and trace calibration). - **lean-checker**: Blind, read only checker that tests an artifact against stated acceptance criteria and returns PASS or FAIL per criterion with evidence. - **lean-coder**: Implements a single, well-scoped code change from an already-approved brief (goal, acceptance criteria, proof command). Use for one bounded edit — not for open-ended exploration, planning, or anything needing web search or MCP data tools. - **lean-coder**: Lean coder. Keeps the codebase as small as it can be without losing safety. Checks the plan before building (is each task needed, can existing code, the standard library or the platform do it) and reviews built code for anything that can be removed or written in fewer lines. Use after the architect writes tasks.md, and in Verify alongside the code-reviewer. ([jagdish3135/knit](https://github.com/jagdish3135/knit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lean-coder.md) · ★1)
- **lean-coder**: Token-efficient coding agent for any project. Use for edits, refactors and bug fixes (multi-file or unfamiliar code) where reading raw files, logs or command output would burn context. Routes work through code-review-graph (structure), token-savior (symbols, memory), context-mode (big-output sandbox, indexed search) and rtk (compact shell output). Returns the same answer quality as a normal agent with far fewer tokens read. ([rohithkh4b3kr3/multi-subagents](https://github.com/rohithkh4b3kr3/multi-subagents/blob/HEAD/agents/lean-coder.md) · ★1)
- **lean-collector**: Collector stage with a shell for read CLIs. Runs without CLAUDE.md (omitClaudeMd, the same omission lean-reader measured in dotfiles#1221) and preloads the data-source-clis skill. Use when a workflow stage must run a read CLI (structural search, raw web content, gh or git facts, a data-source query) and return a report, and the prompt carries every fact it needs. File-only stages stay lean-reader; stages that change anything (edit files, commit, push, post, send) stay general-purpose, because they need the harness rules this agent omits. - **lean-crew-builder**: Surgical 1-2 file edit. Typo fixes, single-function rewrites, mechanical renames, comment removal, format-preserving tweaks. Hard refuses 3+ file scope. Returns lean diff receipt. Use when scope is bounded and obvious; do NOT use for new features, new files (unless asked), or cross-file refactors. ([utk2103/Prompt-Studio](https://github.com/utk2103/Prompt-Studio/blob/HEAD/agents/lean-crew-builder.md) · ★9)
- **lean-crew-investigator**: Read-only code locator. Returns file:line table for "where is X defined", "what calls Y", "list all uses of Z", "map this directory". Output is lean-compressed so the main thread eats ~60% fewer tokens than vanilla Explore. Refuses to suggest fixes. ([utk2103/Prompt-Studio](https://github.com/utk2103/Prompt-Studio/blob/HEAD/agents/lean-crew-investigator.md) · ★9)
- **lean-crew-reviewer**: Diff/branch/file reviewer. One line per finding, severity-tagged, no praise, no scope creep. Output format `path:line: &lt;emoji&gt; &lt;severity&gt;: &lt;problem&gt;. &lt;fix&gt;.` Use for "review this PR", "review my diff", "audit this file". Skips formatting nits unless they change meaning. ([utk2103/Prompt-Studio](https://github.com/utk2103/Prompt-Studio/blob/HEAD/agents/lean-crew-reviewer.md) · ★9)
- **lean-critic**: Harsh, evidence-backed Opus 5.5 critic for work another model or loop wrote: code, scripts, prompts, tickets, docs, PR and issue bodies. Cuts verbosity and duplication, and reports the defects it finds while reading (contradictory instructions, dead instructions the harness cannot honor, vacuous checks, stale references, ungraded inputs a worker can edit). Read-only; every finding cites file:line and gives the exact cut or replacement. Give it a scope: paths, a diff range, PR or issue numbers. Not a bug hunter for logic (use /code-review) and not a plan reviewer (use plan-reviewer). ([SamyakJhaveri/loam](https://github.com/SamyakJhaveri/loam/blob/HEAD/cultivation/marketplace/sam-cc-setup/agents/lean-critic.md))

- **lean-implementer**: Focused, narrowly-scoped code-writing agent for a single implementation task. Edits code, runs the test suite, reports. Its tool set is deliberately minimal: every MCP tool schema a subagent loads is paid for as a cache WRITE on first use (~12.5x read price), so the tens of thousands of tokens of Slack / Jira / cloud / browser schemas a file-editing agent never calls are pure cost. Restricting tools here cuts the per-spawn baseline sharply. Do NOT use this agent for browser/GUI verification — it has no such tools; use a general agent for that. - **legacy-decoder**: 주석 없음·변수명 불분명·매크로 다수·암묵적 컨벤션 같은 레거시 코드를 분석해 의도와 동작을 사람이 읽을 수 있는 형태로 역공학하는 에이전트다. 원작자가 떠난 코드를 근거 있는 해석으로 분해하고, 아키텍트·개발자·업무담당자 관점별 요약과 Mermaid 처리 흐름도를 함께 낸다. 해독만 하며 코드를 수정하거나 리팩터링을 제안하지 않는다. 수정은 safe-modify·scaffold-feature의 몫이다. ([Malburi/AX-NAVI-CLI](https://github.com/Malburi/AX-NAVI-CLI/blob/HEAD/docs/site/agents/legacy-decoder.md) · 2 copies)

"이 코드 뭐하는 거야?", "이 PL/SQL 해석해줘", "이 COBOL 모듈 설명" 등 요청 시 직접 호출. 코드를 수정하거나 리팩터링하지 않으며 결과는 `_workspace/reports/decoded_&lt;slug&gt;.md`에 기록한다.
